Sep 18, 2026: Li Auto Expands Focus to Technology Sales, Following Peers Nio and Xpeng

Li Auto Inc. is reportedly pivoting its strategy to include technology sales, joining fellow Chinese electric vehicle manufacturers Nio and Xpeng in this pursuit. On the 18th of September 2026, local media outlet CnEVPost reported that Li Auto aims to operate its chip and silicon carbide businesses independently while seeking external customers.

Li Auto's management has approved a spinoff of its chip business, with efforts to develop external business opportunities continuing in parallel. Sep 12, 2026: Li Auto Set to Enter European Market Ahead of Schedule

Li Auto Inc. is poised to launch its first vehicle in Europe, the Li 6, at the upcoming Paris Motor Show next month. This marks a significant shift in strategy, as it plans to start selling in Europe in the fourth quarter of this year, a full six years ahead of the timeline previously set by its founder, Li Xiang. The announcement comes on the heels of a competitive automotive landscape in Europe, where multiple Chinese brands, including BYD and XPeng, are also vying for market share.

The Li 6 will be featured prominently at the Paris Motor Show, scheduled for 13th October 2026. However, Li Auto has yet to disclose specific details such as pricing, targeted countries, or sales channels for its European operations. Notably, the company is focusing solely on its battery electric models for this market entry, leaving its extended-range vehicles for markets in the Middle East and Central Asia. This strategic decision aligns with the European Union’s regulations that impose duties on Chinese electric vehicles with range extenders.

The shift away from extended-range vehicles indicates a calculated approach by Li Auto to navigate the complexities of the European automotive market. The EU's duty structure imposes tariffs on vehicles that incorporate range extenders, which generate electricity through gasoline. As a result, only pure battery electric vehicles will be introduced, while the extended-range models will cater to other international markets. This decision reflects not only regulatory considerations but also the current limitations in charging infrastructure across Europe.

Moreover, the company faces uncertainty regarding the tariff rates it will encounter upon entry into the European market. Li Auto is not included among the sampled manufacturers, which may subject it to a range of tariffs from 20.7% to as high as 45.3%, depending on its classification as a cooperating or non-cooperating exporter. Given that the Li 6 starts at approximately $36,000 in China, the added tariffs could position it alongside premium models such as the BMW iX3 in European showrooms, challenging its competitive pricing strategy.

In light of these developments, Li Auto's ambition to establish a foothold in Europe appears both timely and strategic, albeit fraught with challenges. As the company prepares to unveil the Li 6, it remains to be seen how effectively it can capture market share in a continent that is becoming increasingly crowded with electric vehicle offerings from various manufacturers. The upcoming Paris Motor Show will serve as a critical platform for Li Auto to showcase its capabilities and engage with potential customers in this burgeoning market.

- **Coverage Snippet:** November 4th, 2026 (Zacks Investment Research — Delivered by Newstex)

Teradyne TER shares have surged 53.4% in the trailing 12-month period, outperforming the Zacks Computer & Technology sector's rise of 7.5% and the Zacks Electronics - Miscellaneous Products industry's increase of 10%.

TER shares have also outperformed its peers, which include Vertiv VRT, Cisco Systems CSCO and KLA KLAC. All three companies are expanding their footprint in the AI infrastructure space. Vertiv, Cisco Systems and KLA shares have soared 30.5%. 42.4% and 30.9%, respectively, in the trailing 12-month period.

The outperformance can be attributed to robust AI-driven demand across Teradyne's Semiconductor Test, Product Test and Robotics businesses. Strong demand for high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and DRAM testing solutions, AI-related compute, data center power management, high-speed interconnect testing and HDD storage solutions has been a key catalyst. These factors are helping Teradyne fend off competitors such as Vertiv, Cisco Systems and KLA.

TER Stock Performance

Image Source: Zacks Investment Research

Teradyne Rides on Strong AI Infrastructure Growth

Teradyne is benefiting from robust demand for semiconductor testing solutions, driven by accelerating investments in artificial intelligence (AI) infrastructure, high-performance computing, and advanced memory technologies. The company's Semiconductor Test segment reported revenues exceeding $1 billion in the third quarter of 2026, representing 77% year-over-year growth. Strength across system-on-chip (SoC), memory and integrated system test (IST) businesses is driving segment performance.

The SoC business generated revenues of $676 million, supported by nearly 50% year-over-year growth in compute revenues in the third quarter of 2026. Increasing adoption of dual-vendor sourcing strategies among hyperscalers and GPU manufacturers is creating opportunities for Teradyne to expand its market share. Automotive and industrial revenues surged nearly 90%, primarily driven by rising demand for power management solutions supporting AI data center infrastructure.

The memory testing business achieved record revenues of $301 million, benefiting from robust demand for HBM, DRAM testing solutions and recovering NAND investments. The introduction of the Magnum E2 next-generation DRAM tester is expected to strengthen Teradyne's competitive positioning. Meanwhile, IST revenues surged approximately 150% year over year to a record $95 million in the third quarter of 2026, supported by AI-driven demand for hard disk drive testing.

Teradyne's expanding UltraFLEXplus portfolio, increasing semiconductor testing complexity and continued capacity investments position the company to capitalize on sustained AI infrastructure spending. Strengthening customer engagements and anticipated compute testing ramps in 2027 further support its long-term growth prospects.

Teradyne Benefits From Strong Robotics Demand

Teradyne is benefiting from strong demand for robotics solutions, driven by increasing adoption of automation technologies across electronics manufacturing, semiconductor production and AI data center infrastructure. Growing investments in physical AI and robot-assisted assembly and testing are creating significant opportunities for the company's Robotics business.

In the third quarter of 2026, Teradyne's Robotics revenues increased 28% year over year to $96 million, marking its highest third-quarter revenues on record. The segment also achieved profitability during the quarter, reflecting improving demand and operational performance. Electronics and semiconductor manufacturing has emerged as the largest vertical for Robotics, supported by growing automation requirements in complex server and rack assembly processes.

Teradyne's Universal Robots Gen 7 collaborative robot platform and PolyScope X software are strengthening its competitive position. These solutions are designed to support physical AI applications and improve automation flexibility across electronics assembly operations.

The company is also expanding its physical AI ecosystem through partnerships with NVIDIA, Analog Devices, Scale AI, Generalist, Bright Machines and Sereact. These collaborations are expected to accelerate the adoption of intelligent robotics solutions across industrial applications.

Teradyne expects continued Robotics revenue growth in the fourth quarter of 2026. Broader automation deployments by a major customer are expected to support growth in 2027, while emerging physical AI applications could create additional opportunities in 2028 and beyond.

TER Initiates Positive Q4 Guidance

Teradyne's expanding portfolio and strong demand for AI-related applications are expected to drive the company's top-line growth.

For the fourth quarter of 2026, Teradyne expects revenues between $1.25 billion and $1.35 billion. The midpoint of the guidance range stands at $1.30 billion. The Zacks Consensus Estimate for fourth-quarter 2026 revenues is pegged at $1.31 billion, suggesting a 20.75% year-over-year increase.

Non-GAAP earnings are expected to be between $1.95 and $2.25 per share. The consensus mark for earnings is pegged at $2.08 per share, which has increased 2.46% over the past 30 days. This indicates growth of 15.56% on a year-over-year basis.

SECTION 1 RECENT NEWS & PAST YEAR TOP 10 STORIES

1.1 RECENT NEWS

Recent News 1 of 2, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 310 words

Mon Oct 19, 2026: Kingspan Group Expands DeLand Headquarters with Sustainable Retrofit

Kingspan Group plc has completed a significant expansion of its North American headquarters in DeLand, Florida, transforming the facility into a 29,000-square-foot space. This project, finalized on 19th October 2026, has introduced new offices, shared workstations, and a dedicated client experience area to the campus that has served as Kingspan's operational base for over a decade.

The retrofitting effort was notably driven by sustainability objectives, with an emphasis on environmental responsibility throughout the construction process. The newly expanded facility features an all-electric design, eliminating the use of natural gas systems, and includes 12 electric vehicle charging stations. As reported by The Business Journals, this expansion is part of Kingspan's strategy to enhance its operational footprint in North America.

Substantial physical improvements were made during the retrofit, including the installation of over 21,000 square feet of insulated metal wall panels and 30,000 square feet of insulated metal roof panels. In addition to new office spaces, the project has created break rooms and shared workstations, along with an updated client experiential area, as detailed by perusglassmag.com.

In terms of sustainability metrics, Kingspan's decision to retain much of the existing structure has led to a 60% reduction in embodied carbon compared to a complete demolition and rebuild. Approximately 99% of construction waste was diverted from landfills through recycling, reuse, or donation. The headquarters is on track to achieve LEED v4 BD+C Gold Certification and the International Living Future Institute's Net Zero Carbon + Energy Certification, according to roofingcontractor.com.

Since establishing its North American hub in DeLand in 2013, Kingspan Group has consistently invested in enhancing its operations in the region. Alongside the DeLand expansion, the company has also inaugurated a new manufacturing facility in Mattoon, Illinois, indicating a broader strategy to bolster manufacturing capacity beyond its Florida base. The investment in both locations underscores Kingspan's commitment to growth within the North American market.

Recent News 2 of 2, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 284 words

Fri Aug 14, 2026: Kingspan Group plc Completes Landmark Acquisition of BMC Manufacturing

Kingspan Group plc has completed the acquisition of BMC Manufacturing in a landmark transaction valued at €900 million. This acquisition is significant as it marks the largest acquisition of a private Irish company by another Irish firm, highlighting the robust capabilities of Irish enterprises in the competitive M&A landscape.

The deal was officially announced on the 14th of August, 2026, and underscores the strategic growth ambitions of Kingspan Group plc, a global leader in high-performance insulation and building envelope solutions. BMC Manufacturing specializes in designing and manufacturing low voltage switchgear and critical power management solutions for the data centre industry.

James Toomey, M&A Advisory Lead at Deloitte, who facilitated the transaction, commented, "This is a landmark transaction as the largest ever acquisition of a private Irish company by an Irish buyer. It reflects the strength of Ireland Inc. that two Irish companies can transact at this scale." He further added that the partnership with Kingspan will significantly benefit BMC Manufacturing, accelerating its growth plans, particularly in the US market.

Deloitte played a pivotal role in this transaction, serving as the lead M&A advisor to the shareholders of BMC Manufacturing. The advisory firm provided comprehensive services across various domains, including financial vendor due diligence and tax structuring, ensuring a smooth transaction process. Brendan Meehan, Founder and Chairman of BMC Manufacturing, acknowledged the invaluable support from Deloitte, stating that their expertise facilitated navigating the complexities of the deal.

The acquisition, pending regulatory approval, is expected to finalize in the fourth quarter of 2026. This development not only represents a significant milestone for Kingspan Group plc but also sets a precedent for further M&A activity within the Irish market, showcasing the resilience and ambition of domestic companies.

1.2 PAST YEAR TOP 10 STORIES

Story 1 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 70 words

Data-centre power specialist acquired in transaction of up to 900 million euro

Alliance News and RTE reported an initial 850 million euro purchase of BMC Manufacturing, plus up to 50 million euro contingent, funded by 600 million euro cash and 250 million euro of new shares. BMC 2026 revenue is indicated near 280 million euro and EBITDA near 90 million euro. Completion is expected in late 2026 subject to clearance. The deal extends Advnsys into power systems, with integration and earn-out risk.

Story 2 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 73 words

Record full-year revenue of 9.2 billion euro and trading profit of 955 million euro

Official results disclosed revenue of 9.2 billion euro, up 7 percent, and trading profit of 955 million euro, up 5 percent. Advnsys sales rose 12 percent with backlog 24 percent ahead. Acquisitions added 8 percent of sales growth. Trading margin eased to 10.4 percent and net debt rose to 1,884 million euro. Management indicated a path toward about 1.05 billion euro of trading profit in the following year, subject to mixed construction demand.

Story 3 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 65 words

First-half profit rises 10 percent as full-year targets are lifted toward 10 billion euro of sales

RTE reported first-half revenue of 4.858 billion euro, up 8 percent, and trading profit of 487 million euro, up 10 percent. Advnsys sales were indicated up 34 percent. Management stated that full-year revenue can reasonably exceed 10 billion euro and lifted trading-profit guidance to about 1.125 billion euro. Currency and listing-review costs reduced the print. Second-half delivery is assessed as critical to the raised target.

Story 4 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 58 words

Minority listing of data-infrastructure unit considered on Euronext Amsterdam

An official announcement and Reuters reporting indicated consideration of a 25 percent Amsterdam listing of Advnsys while retaining majority control and consolidation. Advnsys 2024 EBITDA was cited at 197 million euro on 1.48 billion euro of revenue. Davy and Goodbody commentary pointed to a possible 6 billion euro unit value. The plan was exploratory, market-dependent and later withdrawn.

Story 5 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 65 words

Planned minority listing of data-infrastructure unit withdrawn in favour of full ownership

Reuters and RTE reported that the Advnsys offering would not proceed. Management cited stronger momentum toward at least doubling core profit over four to five years and a preference to keep the order book inside the group. The Annual Report treated the decision as a non-adjusting subsequent event. Holders retain full upside and full capex burden, forgoing a minority listing that had drawn investor interest.

Story 6 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Construction starts on 280 million euro manufacturing campus near Lviv

The company newsroom announced a first permit for a 280 million euro low-carbon campus near Lviv, expected to support more than 700 jobs within a five-year build. Output is indicated as insulation, building materials and district heating for reconstruction. UkraineInvest and project counsel confirmed government engagement. Security, permitting and timeline risk remain high. Near-term earnings contribution is assessed as limited.

Story 7 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 63 words

Remaining shares in Nordic waterproofing platform taken private after 90 percent threshold

Irish Times and Irish Examiner reporting, plus the official offer outcome, showed the holding rise through 90 percent to 98.14 percent, triggering squeeze-out and delisting. Annual Report total consideration was 451.6 million euro including acquired debt. Nordic is a Northern European waterproofing platform with about 1,300 staff. Nordic construction weakness and modest historic operating profit frame the return risk on the accumulated outlay.

Story 8 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 60 words

Central European daylighting and smoke-ventilation operations added for 99.7 million euro

Group accounts disclosed a 99.7 million euro combined price for Mercor daylighting and smoke-ventilation activities completed in October 2025 across eight countries. The seller cited a maximum 420 million zloty including a contingent slice. The deal was not individually material to group results. It is interpreted as an Advnsys European bolt-on with integration and earn-out measurement risk through March 2026.

Story 9 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 66 words

Majority holding taken in Argentine structural insulated-panel manufacturer

RTE reported a first Argentine acquisition via a majority stake in Grupo LTN, a panel maker with more than 377 staff and plants in Mendoza and Buenos Aires. Latin America was indicated at about 1,700 employees and 500 million euro of annualised revenue. Price and LTN earnings were not disclosed. The move is assessed as a coherent local manufacturing entry with elevated macro and currency risk.

Story 10 of 10, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 64 words

First-quarter sales hold up as data-infrastructure unit rises 28 percent

The April trading update, as reported by RTE and the Irish Examiner, showed sales of 2.1 billion euro, up 3 percent pre-currency, despite winter disruption. Advnsys sales were up 28 percent pre-currency. Envelope sales were 2 percent behind. Guidance for full-year trading profit remained about 1.05 billion euro, with Kentucky and United States roofing plants still on schedule. Mix quality was not fully disclosed.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

Kingspan Group plc, an Irish-based multinational, is a leading provider of high-performance insulation and building envelope solutions, specializing in energy-efficient products for residential, commercial, and industrial applications. The company's core activities encompass the design, manufacture, and distribution of insulation panels, roofing systems, offsite construction modules, and sustainable technologies such as solar thermal systems and water management solutions. With a strong emphasis on innovation and environmental sustainability, Kingspan has seen rapid growth in segments like data center infrastructure and renewable energy products, driven by increasing demand for green building practices and energy conservation globally. This expansion has bolstered the company's presence across Europe, North America, and Asia, positioning it as a key player in the construction and energy efficiency sectors, while maintaining a commitment to reducing carbon footprints through advanced materials and technologies. It is the International Depository Receipt (IDR) market's 7th largest Building materials company by market capitalization.

- **Coverage Snippet:** XPENG INC ("XPEV-N") - Appoints Korea Managing Director, Confirms Plans to Enter South - Korean Market //st * XPENG today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik, a 30-year automotive industry veteran with a proven track record in sales and commercial operations, as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, effective December 1, 2026. * The appointment marks XPENG's planned entry into the South Korean market. Details on launch timing and local business operations will be announced at a later date.

XPeng Inc. ("XPENG" or "the Company), a leading global Physical AI company, today announced the appointment of Lee Man-sik as Managing Director of XPENG Motors Korea, as the company prepares to bring its smart vehicles to one of Asia's most sophisticated automotive markets.

Mr. Lee brings more than three decades of automotive industry experience. Having begun his career in engineering, he has built expertise across sales, marketing, product strategy, brand communications, dealer network development and customer service. He joined Volvo Cars Korea in 2003 and played a pivotal role in establishing Volvo as a leading premium import brand in South Korea.

At Volvo Cars Korea, he led the development and execution of the brand's mid- to long-term growth strategy, with responsibilities spanning brand positioning, product planning, sales and marketing, nationwide dealer expansion and customer experience.

"Over the past thirty years, I have learned that sustainable growth for a premium brand rests not only on outstanding products but on trust built with customers," said Mr. Lee. "XPENG is a brand presenting new possibilities for the industry through innovative AI technology and a vision for future mobility. I am delighted to join this journey of change."

On the Korean market, he added: "Korean customers hold exceptionally high expectations for automobiles and are remarkably open to new technology and innovation. We believe XPENG's advanced technology and product competitiveness can offer them new choices and differentiated value. Above all, our goal is to build a brand customers can choose with confidence one that delivers a trustworthy experience at every step of ownership."

In his new role, Mr. Lee will lead XPENG's efforts to establish its business foundation in South Korea, drawing on his deep understanding of the local automotive market and extensive experience in premium brand operations. His remit will include developing sales and service networks, building strategic partnerships and strengthening XPENG's brand presence in the market.

XPENG's planned entry into South Korea comes as the company continues to deepen its international footprint this year. In October 2026, the company delivered 50,237 vehicles worldwide up 22% from September and 20% from a year earlier, a new monthly record. Overseas deliveries climbed to 13,326 units, surging 166% year on year to set a record of their own. The company now operates across 83 countries and regions, supported by eight R&D centers, six manufacturing bases and more than 1,200 stores worldwide. At the 2026 Paris Motor Show in October, XPENG launched its next-gen AI flagship SUV, the G9L, for the European market and showcased its latest intelligent driving technologies, while expanding opportunities for global technology partnerships. XPENG aims to bring that same differentiated technology and customer experience to South Korea, and details on launch timing and local operations will be shared in due course.

About XPENG

XPENG is a leading global Physical AI company, dedicated to bringing artificial intelligence into the physical world to reshape future mobility and smart living. Through in-house R&D, XPENG has developed a full-stack Physical AI architecture spanning Turing AI chips, world foundation models, and highly integrated software and hardware applications. This unified technology foundation of XPENG powers an expansive product portfolio of smart EVs, robotaxis, and humanoid robots, advancing the deployment of Physical AI at scale. Headquartered in Guangzhou, China, XPENG is dual-primary listed on the New York Stock Exchange and the Hong Kong Stock Exchange. With global capabilities across R&D, manufacturing, sales, and services, XPENG drives continuous technological innovation and fosters an open Physical AI ecosystem, making life smarter, safer, and better for users worldwide. For more information, please visit https://www.xpeng.com/.

- **Syndicated URLs:** [link](https://www.tradingview.com/news/dpa_afx:f502c73537398:0-asian-markets-trade-mostly-lower/), [link](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60773703362&p=21l&v=1&x=97IHWamss-tPcacfWqTqYA)
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Agent Reliability Engineering (ARE)
- **Matched Competitors:** Appen, TELUS Digital, Sama, TaskUs, CloudFactory, iMerit
- **Coverage Snippet:** Asian markets are mostly lower on Wednesday, following the mixed cues from Wall Street overnight, with markets in Japan on return after a holiday and South Korea tumbling amid anxiety over AI industry and unresolved geopolitical risks. Traders also remain cautious ahead to the U.S. midterm election results. Rising expectations on a possible settlement between the U.S. and Iran is aiding market sentiment, with crude oil prices easing. Asian markets closed mixed on Tuesday.

U.S. forces conducted secret attacks on Iran's Qeshm Island yesterday. The strikes targeted a newly constructed anti-aircraft facility near the Strait of Hormuz. The attacks could be a measure to stop Iran from attacking commercial ships in the Strait of Hormuz.

Iran's President Masoud Pezeshkian stated that renewed fighting will benefit no one, and reiterated that Iran wishes to solve the dispute through negotiations.

Qatar said it saw hope for an interim deal as both the U.S. and Iran evaluate new proposals put forward by mediators, despite fresh U.S. strikes and continued threats to shipping through Hormuz.

However, Iran's national security chief indicated in a social media post that Washington's response to Iran's demands for reopening the Strait of Hormuz and resuming peace talks was insufficient.

Australian shares are trading notably lower on Wednesday, reversing the gains in the previous session, with the benchmark S&P/ASX 200 falling below the 8,850 level, following the mixed cues from Wall Street overnight, with weakness in mining and financial stocks partially offset by gains in energy and technology stocks.

The benchmark S&P/ASX 200 Index is losing 44.30 points or 0.50 percent to 8,842.50, after hitting a low of 8,820.30 earlier. The broader All Ordinaries Index is down 44.80 points or 0.50 percent to 8,964.10. Australian stocks ended modestly higher on Tuesday.

Among major miners, BHP Group and Rio Tinto are losing almost 1 percent each, while Mineral Resources and Fortescue are edging down 0.2 percent each.

Oil stocks are mostly higher. Woodside Energy, Beach energy and Santos are gaining almost 1 percent each, while and Origin Energy is adding almost 2 percent.

In the tech space, Afterpay owner Block is surging almost 7 percent on upbeat quarterly results, while Appen and WiseTech Global are gaining almost 2 percent each. Zip and Xero are edging down 0.3 percent each.

Among the big four banks, Commonwealth Bank and ANZ Banking are losing more than 1 percent each, while Westpac and National Australia bank are down almost 1 percent each.

Among gold miners, Evolution Mining is edging up 0.1 percent and Genesis Minerals is gaining more than 1 percent, while Resolute Mining is losing more than 1 percent and Northern Star Resources is edging down 0.5 percent. Newmont is flat.

In economic news, the services sector in Australia fell into contraction territory in October, the latest survey from S&P Global revealed on Wednesday with a services PMI score of 48.5. That's down from 51.9 in September, and it slips beneath the boom-or-bust line of 50 that separates expansion from contraction. The survey also showed that the composite index fell to 48.2 in October from 51.3 in September.

Meanwhile, Australian household spending rose 0.6 percent on month in September 2026, rebounding from a downwardly revised 0.1 percent decline in the prior month and marking its seventh monthly increase this year. On an annual basis, household spending growth accelerated to 6.8 percent from 6.5 percent in August.

In the currency market, the Aussie dollar is trading at $0.696 on Wednesday.

The Japanese stock market is trading sharply lower on Wednesday, extending the losses in the previous session, following the mixed cues from Wall Street overnight. The Nikkei 225 is tumbling 3.6 percent to below the 60,850 level, with weakness across most sectors led by financial and technology stocks.

The benchmark Nikkei 225 Index closed the morning session at 60,835.36, down 2,246.50 points or 3.56 percent, after hitting a low of 60,799.11 earlier. Japanese stocks ended modestly lower on Monday ahead of the holiday on Tuesday.

Market heavyweight SoftBank Group is tumbling almost 8 percent, while Uniqlo operator Fast Retailing is adding more than 1 percent. Among automakers, Honda is declining almost 2 percent and Toyota is losing more than 2 percent.

In the tech space, Advantest is tumbling almost 7 percent, Tokyo Electron is slipping almost 6 percent and Screen Holdings is declining more than 5 percent.

In the banking sector, Sumitomo Mitsui Financial and Mizuho Financial are losing more than 3 percent each, while Mitsubishi UFJ Financial is declining almost 3 percent.

Among the major exporters, Mitsubishi Electric is edging down 0.4 percent, Canon is losing almost 1 percent and Panasonic is tumbling almost 8 percent, while Sony is edging up 0.1 percent.

Among other major losers, Mitsui Chemicals and Taiyo Yuden are plunging more than 9 percent each, while Kioxia Holdings and Kyocera are tumbling more than 7 percent each. Socionext, Disco and Furukawa Electric are slipping almost 6 percent each, while Fujikura is declining more than 5 percent, while Sumitomo Electric Industries and Kokusai Electric are sliding almost 5 percent. JX Advanced Metals, Mitsui Kinzoku, Resonac Holdings and Renesas Electronics are losing more than 4 percent each.

Conversely, Yamato Holdings is soaring almost 14 percent, Yamaha is surging more than 5 percent, Japan Airlines is jumping almost 5 percent, Capcom is advancing more than 4 percent and Konami Group is gaining almost 4 percent, while NEXON and Nitto Denko are adding more than 3 percent each. Kirin Holdings, LY, ZOZO, Nisshin Seifun, ANA Holdings, Nintendo and Central Japan Railway are all up almost 3 percent each.

In economic news, the monetary base in Japan was down 13.6 percent on year in October, the Bank of Japan said on Wednesday - coming in at 532.571 trillion yen. That beat forecasts for a decline of 14.0 percent following the 15.2 percent drop in September.

Banknotes in circulation fell 2.3 percent, while coins in circulation sank 1.1 percent and current account balances slumped 16.4 percent - including a 15.2 percent fall in reserve balances. The adjusted monetary base was up 4.9 percent on year at 533.392 trillion yen.

In the currency market, the U.S. dollar is trading in the higher 157 yen-range on Wednesday.

Elsewhere in Asia, Singapore and South Korea are down 1.3 and 2.0 percent, respectively. China, Hong Kong, Malaysia Indonesia and Taiwan are lower by between 0.1 and 0.5 percent each. New Zealand is bucking the trend and is up 0.3 percent.

On Wall Street, stock indexes once again moved in opposite directions during trading on Tuesday after turning mixed over the course of the previous session. While the Nasdaq and S&P 500 rebounded following Monday's significant downturn, the narrower Dow moved back to the downside.

The Nasdaq advanced 207.65 points or 0.8 percent to 25,372.01 and the S&P 500 rose 27.52 points or 0.4 percent to 7,568.92, but the Dow fell 197.26 points or 0.4 percent to 51,786.72.

Meanwhile, the major European markets also moved to the downside on the day. While the U.K.'s FTSE 100 Index slumped 1.1 percent, the German DAX Index fell by 0.5 percent and the French CAC 40 Index dipped by 0.3 percent.

Crude oil prices turned lower on Tuesday amid rising expectations on a possible settlement between the U.S. and Iran. West Texas Intermediate crude for December delivery was down $0.52 or 0.54 percent at $95.94 per barrel.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Recent News 3 of 3, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 283 words

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

- **Coverage Snippet:** TELUS CORP ("T-T;TU-N") - New TELUS collective agreement ratified by the United - Steelworkers membership

TELUS today announced that team members represented by the Telecommunications Workers Union, United Steelworkers Local 1944 (USW) bargaining unit have ratified the new agreement reached by the parties earlier this month. This new agreement, which has a term of April 1, 2027 to December 31, 2031, will apply to more than 3,000 TELUS team members nationally.

"Thank you to our team members for supporting this agreement and to both bargaining teams for their constructive work," said Victor Dodig, President and CEO of TELUS. "Reaching an agreement well ahead of the current contract's expiry provides stability for our team members and our business, allowing us to focus together on delivering the reliable service and outstanding experiences our customers count on."

About TELUS

TELUS (TSX: T, NYSE: TU) is a leading Canadian communications technology company operating in more than 45 countries and generating over $20 billion in annual revenue with more than 17 million customer connections through our advanced suite of broadband services for consumers, businesses and the public sector. TELUS is passionate about putting our customers and communities first, leading the way globally in client service excellence and social capitalism. The TELUS PureFibre and 5G networks connect Canadians at home, at work and in the communities where they live.

TELUS Health is enhancing approximately 159 million lives across 200 countries and territories through innovative preventive medicine and well-being technologies. TELUS Agriculture &Consumer Goods utilizes digital technologies and data insights to optimize the connection between producers and consumers. TELUS Digital specializes in digital customer experiences and future-focused digital transformations that deliver value for their global clients.

Since 2000, TELUS and our team members have contributed more than $1.85 billion in cash, in-kind contributions, time and programs to communities across Canada and around the world.

For more information, visit telus.com.

Wed Sep 30, 2026: ExlService Reports Significant Sustainability Advances in Latest Report

ExlService Holdings, Inc. [NASDAQ: EXLS] has published its seventh annual Sustainability Report, titled 'Embedding Sustainability,' showcasing significant advancements in corporate social responsibility (CSR), emissions reductions, and responsible AI governance. The report, released on 30th September 2026, highlights EXL's commitment to integrating sustainability into its core business practices as a means to enhance long-term value for stakeholders.

In the report, dated 2026-10-01 16:41:39.309490, EXL reveals a remarkable 64% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 greenhouse gas emissions from its 2019 baseline. This achievement is complemented by an impressive 78% of the global workforce participating in various CSR initiatives, reflecting the company's dedication to fostering a culture of community engagement and environmental stewardship.

Ajay Ayyappan, executive vice president and general counsel at EXL, stated, 'At EXL, sustainability is not a side initiative; it is central to how we build a stronger, more resilient business.' He emphasized that the report embodies the company's ethos of continuous improvement, demonstrating measurable progress across all facets of its sustainability agenda, including a robust increase in community investment and the maturation of its Responsible and Secure AI program.

The 2025 Sustainability Report adheres to the Global Reporting Initiative Standards and the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board Software and IT Services Standard, ensuring transparency and accountability. Additionally, it aligns with the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial Disclosures and maps to the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals, further solidifying EXL's commitment to global sustainability efforts.

EXL remains focused on propelling enterprises to enhance their business models through data and AI, with an emphasis on sustainable practices. As the company continues to innovate and lead in responsible corporate governance, the insights from this report may be of particular interest to investors looking to align their portfolios with companies demonstrating strong environmental, social, and governance (ESG) practices.

Recent News 2 of 3, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 422 words

Wed Sep 9, 2026: EXL Launches ‘Go Beyond’ Brand, Emphasizing Data-Driven Transformation

EXLService Holdings, Inc. (NASDAQ:EXLS) has unveiled its new brand, ‘Go Beyond,' which encapsulates the company's commitment to innovation and transformation in the realms of data and artificial intelligence. This announcement comes as EXL celebrates its 20th year as a publicly traded entity, with the brand launch taking place on 10th September 2026 at a Nasdaq opening bell ceremony. The new branding aims to reflect EXL's unique position at the intersection of data, AI, industry context, and enterprise execution, driving measurable outcomes for its clients.

Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer of EXL, emphasized that ‘Go Beyond' is not just a tagline but a daily standard for the company. He stated, "Our clients come to us to fundamentally reimagine how their enterprises operate. With AI transforming every industry, we've built something rare; a company with domain depth to know what questions to ask and data and AI capabilities to answer them at scale." This strategic shift aims to further solidify EXL's standing as a leader in leveraging data and AI for business transformation.

The rebranding initiative aligns with EXL's foundational purpose of finding better ways to serve its clients and symbolizes its ongoing growth and adaptability. Since its foray into data and analytics in 2006, EXL has consistently pushed the boundaries of traditional industry norms. This includes the recent acquisition of iMerit in August 2026, which has expanded its capabilities in advanced AI model training and frontier AI development. Today, over 60% of EXL's revenues are derived from data and AI-driven services, showcasing its market-leading growth trajectory.

Shirley Macbeth, chief marketing officer of EXL, remarked, "For 27 years, EXL has been built on a simple idea: We find a better way. ‘Go Beyond' is the brand that captures that. It's an invitation to our clients and employees to push the boundaries of what's possible and a reflection of the company EXL has become, a global data and AI leader that combines deep domain and context expertise with trusted execution to drive outcomes that matter." This statement underscores EXL's focus on fostering an innovative culture that prioritizes client success.

EXL continues to serve a diverse clientele that includes leading corporations across various sectors such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and capital markets. As the company embarks on this new branding journey, stakeholders will be keenly observing how ‘Go Beyond' translates into tangible outcomes and strategic advantages for clients in an increasingly competitive landscape. For more insights into EXL's transformation and the ‘Go Beyond' brand in action, interested parties can visit the company's official website.

Recent News 3 of 3, Source: HourlyBusinessNews, 283 words

Thu Aug 20, 2026: ExlService Holdings Expands Credit Capacity: A New Era for AI Investments?

ExlService Holdings, Inc. has taken a significant step in its financial strategy by entering into a new US$1.00 billion senior secured credit agreement with a syndicate led by PNC Bank, as announced on August 18, 2026. This move replaces its previous Citibank facility and extends revolving and term loan maturities to August 18, 2031.

The new credit facility is designed to provide ExlService greater balance sheet flexibility, allowing the company to fund acquisitions, technology investments, and share repurchases. It features leverage-linked pricing and covenants that impose defined limits on debt and interest coverage, which enhances the company's ability to pursue its ambitious goals in the AI sector.

As ExlService Holdings pivots from traditional outsourcing to a focus on data and AI, the new US$1.00 billion credit line appears to support the ongoing narrative of AI-driven growth. This expanded credit capacity is expected to bolster ExlService's strategy to secure AI-led deal wins while managing the associated risks, particularly those arising from rising wage inflation and compliance costs, which could complicate pricing power.

The recent acquisition of iMerit, which has brought its CEO into ExlService's executive team, further underscores the importance of scaling AI data services. Together with the larger credit facility, this acquisition highlights the company's commitment to enhancing its capabilities in this high-demand sector, while also raising concerns related to execution risks in integrating new talent and maintaining resilient operating margins.

Analysts are optimistic about ExlService Holdings' growth potential, projecting revenues of approximately US$3.2 billion and earnings of around US$374.5 million by 2029. The new credit facility could reinforce this growth outlook; however, if cost pressures intensify more than anticipated, it may lead to a reassessment of the feasibility of meeting these targets.

SECTION 2 CORPORATE PROFILE

2.1 Activities

ExlService Holdings, Inc., a Nasdaq-listed company, is a global leader in data analytics, digital operations, and consulting services, primarily serving industries such as insurance, healthcare, banking, and retail. The company's core business revolves around leveraging advanced analytics, artificial intelligence, and machine learning to optimize operations, enhance decision-making, and drive efficiency for its clients. By transforming complex data into actionable insights, ExlService helps organizations streamline processes and mitigate risks. Among its fastest-growing segments are analytics-driven solutions and digital transformation services, fueled by increasing demand for AI-powered tools and cloud-based platforms. This focus has enabled the company to expand its client base and achieve strong revenue growth, positioning it as a key player in the evolving landscape of business process outsourcing and technology-enabled services. ExlService maintains a commitment to innovation and client-centric strategies, fostering sustainable growth in a competitive market. It is the NASDAQ's 6th largest Business services company by market capitalization.

### 74. [TaskUs (TASK) Beats Q3 Earnings and Revenue Estimates](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60779723920&p=21l&v=1&x=SNvtd8Lsf4aYhaHphuYD1g)
- **Outlet(s):** Newstex Blogs Zacks Investment Research | **Published:** `2026-11-05` | **Sentiment:** `POSITIVE` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `0.60`
- **Matched Competitors:** Appen, TELUS Digital, Sama, TaskUs, CloudFactory, iMerit
- **Coverage Snippet:** November 4th, 2026 (Zacks Investment Research — Delivered by Newstex)

TaskUs (TASK) came out with quarterly earnings of $0.34 per share, beating the Zacks Consensus Estimate of $0.3 per share. This compares to earnings of $0.42 per share a year ago. These figures are adjusted for non-recurring items.

This quarterly report represents an earnings surprise of +13.33%. A quarter ago, it was expected that this provider of outsourced digital services would post earnings of $0.29 per share when it actually produced earnings of $0.33, delivering a surprise of +13.79%.

Over the last four quarters, the company has surpassed consensus EPS estimates three times.

TaskUs, which belongs to the Zacks Computers - IT Services industry, posted revenues of $314.75 million for the quarter ended September 2026, surpassing the Zacks Consensus Estimate by 3.02%. This compares to year-ago revenues of $298.71 million. The company has topped consensus revenue estimates four times over the last four quarters.

The sustainability of the stock's immediate price movement based on the recently-released numbers and future earnings expectations will mostly depend on management's commentary on the earnings call.

TaskUs shares have lost about 29.4% since the beginning of the year versus the S&P 500's gain of 10.6%.

What's Next for TaskUs?

While TaskUs has underperformed the market so far this year, the question that comes to investors' minds is: what's next for the stock?

There are no easy answers to this key question, but one reliable measure that can help investors address this is the company's earnings outlook. Not only does this include current consensus earnings expectations for the coming quarter(s), but also how these expectations have changed lately.

Empirical research shows a strong correlation between near-term stock movements and trends in earnings estimate revisions. Investors can track such revisions by themselves or rely on a tried-and-tested rating tool like the Zacks Rank, which has an impressive track record of harnessing the power of earnings estimate revisions.

Ahead of this earnings release, the estimate revisions trend for TaskUs was mixed. While the magnitude and direction of estimate revisions could change following the company's just-released earnings report, the current status translates into a Zacks Rank #3 (Hold) for the stock. So, the shares are expected to perform in line with the market in the near future. You can see the complete list of today's Zacks #1 Rank (Strong Buy) stocks here.

It will be interesting to see how estimates for the coming quarters and the current fiscal year change in the days ahead. The current consensus EPS estimate is $0.33 on $321.14 million in revenues for the coming quarter and $1.30 on $1.24 billion in revenues for the current fiscal year.

Investors should be mindful of the fact that the outlook for the industry can have a material impact on the performance of the stock as well. In terms of the Zacks Industry Rank, Computers - IT Services is currently in the top 46% of the 250 plus Zacks industries. Our research shows that the top 50% of the Zacks-ranked industries outperform the bottom 50% by a factor of more than 2 to 1.

One other stock from the same industry, C3.ai, Inc. (AI), is yet to report results for the quarter ended October 2026.

This company is expected to post quarterly loss of $0.22 per share in its upcoming report, which represents a year-over-year change of +12%. The consensus EPS estimate for the quarter has been revised 0.3% lower over the last 30 days to the current level.

C3.ai, Inc.'s revenues are expected to be $53.09 million, down 29.4% from the year-ago quarter.
